from sqlalchemy import Column, String, DateTime, Text, Integer, Enum as SQLEnum
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from database import Base
import enum


class JobStatus(str, enum.Enum):
    PENDING = "PENDING"
    PROCESSING = "PROCESSING"
    COMPLETED = "COMPLETED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


class Job(Base):
    __tablename__ = "jobs"
    
    id = Column(String, primary_key=True, index=True)
    operation = Column(String, nullable=False, index=True)
    status = Column(SQLEnum(JobStatus), default=JobStatus.PENDING, nullable=False, index=True)
    input_files = Column(Text, nullable=False)  # JSON string
    output_file = Column(String, nullable=True)
    error = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    progress = Column(Integer, default=0)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, server_default=func.now(), nullable=False)
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, onupdate=func.now(), nullable=False)
    
    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "operation": self.operation,
            "status": self.status.value if isinstance(self.status, JobStatus) else self.status,
            "input_files": self.input_files,
            "output_file": self.output_file,
            "error": self.error,
            "progress": self.progress,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat() if self.created_at else None,
            "updated_at": self.updated_at.isoformat() if self.updated_at else None
        }

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
import os
import sys
sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from services import storage_service


router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/download/{filename}")
async def download_file(filename: str):
    """
    Download a processed output file.
    """
    outputs_path = "C:\\Users\\ADIL\\Documents\\pj\\storage\\outputs"
    file_path = os.path.join(outputs_path, filename)
    
    if not os.path.exists(file_path):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="File not found")
    
    return FileResponse(
        path=file_path,
        filename=filename,
        media_type='application/pdf'
    )

    status: str
    message: str
    version: str

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
import sys
import os
sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from services import job_service


router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}")
async def get_job(job_id: str):
    """
    Get job status and details by job ID.
    Returns job metadata including status, input files, and output file if completed.
    """
    job = job_service.get_job(job_id)
    
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Job not found")
    
    return {
        "success": True,
        "job": job.to_dict()
    }

from fastapi import APIRouter, UploadFile, File, Form, HTTPException, Depends
from typing import List
import sys
import os
import json
sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from services import job_service, storage_service, queue_service, JobStatus
from database import get_db
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from models import Job as JobModel


router = APIRouter()


@router.post("/upload")
async def upload_file(
    operation: str = Form(...),
    files: List[UploadFile] = File(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    Upload PDF file(s) for processing.
    
    Validates that uploaded files are PDFs using magic bytes and extension.
    Sanitizes filenames to prevent path traversal.
    Stores files in storage/uploads/.
    Registers job in database and enqueues to Redis.
    Returns consistent JSON response.
    """
    if not files:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No files provided")
    
    # Validate operation
    valid_operations = ["merge", "split", "compress", "rotate", "organize", "pdf_to_jpg", "jpg_to_pdf", "watermark", "protect", "unlock"]
    if operation not in valid_operations:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=f"Invalid operation. Must be one of: {', '.join(valid_operations)}"
        )
    
    input_files_metadata = []
    
    for upload_file in files:
        # Validate file type - check MIME type
        if not upload_file.content_type or not upload_file.content_type.startswith("application/pdf"):
            if not upload_file.filename or not upload_file.filename.lower().endswith(('.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png')):
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=400,
                    detail=f"File '{upload_file.filename}' is not a valid file type"
                )
        
        # For image files, we don't check PDF magic bytes
        if upload_file.content_type and upload_file.content_type.startswith("application/pdf"):
            # Read and validate magic bytes
            file_content = await upload_file.read()
            if not storage_service.validate_pdf_magic_bytes(file_content):
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=400,
                    detail=f"File '{upload_file.filename}' does not appear to be a valid PDF"
                )
            # Reset file pointer for storage
            await upload_file.seek(0)
    
    # Create job in database
    job = job_service.create_job(
        operation=operation,
        input_files=[]
    )
    
    # Save all files and collect metadata
    for upload_file in files:
        try:
            file_metadata = storage_service.save_upload_file(upload_file, job.job_id)
            input_files_metadata.append({
                "original_name": file_metadata["original_name"],
                "stored_name": file_metadata["stored_name"],
                "size": file_metadata["size"]
            })
        except Exception as e:
            # Cleanup: delete job if file save fails
            job_service.delete_job(job.job_id)
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=500,
                detail=f"Failed to save file '{upload_file.filename}': {str(e)}"
            )
    
    # Update job with file metadata
    job_service.update_job_input_files(job.job_id, input_files_metadata)
    
    # Enqueue job to Redis
    job_data = {
        "job_id": job.job_id,
        "operation": operation,
        "input_files": input_files_metadata
    }
    await queue_service.enqueue_job(job_data)
    
    return {
        "success": True,
        "job_id": job.job_id,
        "status": job.status.value,
        "operation": job.operation,
        "files": input_files_metadata
    }

import uuid
import threading
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, Optional, List
from enum import Enum
import json
import os


class JobStatus(str, Enum):
    PENDING = "PENDING"
    PROCESSING = "PROCESSING"
    COMPLETED = "COMPLETED"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


class Job:
    def __init__(
        self,
        job_id: str,
        operation: str,
        input_files: List[Dict[str, str]],
        status: JobStatus = JobStatus.PENDING,
        output_file: Optional[str] = None,
        error: Optional[str] = None
    ):
        self.job_id = job_id
        self.operation = operation
        self.input_files = input_files
        self.status = status
        self.output_file = output_file
        self.error = error
        self.created_at = datetime.utcnow()
        self.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "job_id": self.job_id,
            "operation": self.operation,
            "input_files": self.input_files,
            "status": self.status.value,
            "output_file": self.output_file,
            "error": self.error,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "updated_at": self.updated_at.isoformat()
        }


class JobService:
    def __init__(self, storage_path: str = "C:\\Users\\ADIL\\Documents\\pj\\storage\\jobs"):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.jobs: Dict[str, Job] = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        os.makedirs(storage_path, exist_ok=True)
        self._load_jobs()

    def _load_jobs(self):
        """Load jobs from disk on startup."""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(self.storage_path):
                for filename in os.listdir(self.storage_path):
                    if filename.endswith('.json'):
                        file_path = os.path.join(self.storage_path, filename)
                        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                            data = json.load(f)
                            job = Job(
                                job_id=data['job_id'],
                                operation=data['operation'],
                                input_files=data['input_files'],
                                status=JobStatus(data['status']),
                                output_file=data.get('output_file'),
                                error=data.get('error')
                            )
                            self.jobs[job.job_id] = job
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error loading jobs: {e}")

    def _save_job(self, job: Job):
        """Persist job to disk."""
        try:
            file_path = os.path.join(self.storage_path, f"{job.job_id}.json")
            with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(job.to_dict(), f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error saving job {job.job_id}: {e}")

    def create_job(
        self,
        operation: str,
        input_files: List[Dict[str, str]]
    ) -> Job:
        """Create a new job with PENDING status."""
        job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        job = Job(
            job_id=job_id,
            operation=operation,
            input_files=input_files,
            status=JobStatus.PENDING
        )
        
        with self.lock:
            self.jobs[job_id] = job
            self._save_job(job)
        
        return job

    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> Optional[Job]:
        """Get a job by ID."""
        with self.lock:
            return self.jobs.get(job_id)

    def update_job_status(
        self,
        job_id: str,
        status: JobStatus,
        output_file: Optional[str] = None,
        error: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Optional[Job]:
        """Update job status atomically."""
        with self.lock:
            job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
            if job:
                job.status = status
                job.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
                if output_file is not None:
                    job.output_file = output_file
                if error is not None:
                    job.error = error
                self._save_job(job)
                return job
        return None

    def update_job_input_files(self, job_id: str, input_files: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> Optional[Job]:
        """Update job input files atomically."""
        with self.lock:
            job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
            if job:
                job.input_files = input_files
                job.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
                self._save_job(job)
                return job
        return None

    def get_pending_jobs(self) -> List[Job]:
        """Get all jobs with PENDING status."""
        with self.lock:
            return [job for job in self.jobs.values() if job.status == JobStatus.PENDING]

    def delete_job(self, job_id: str) -> bool:
        """Delete a job from memory and disk."""
        with self.lock:
            if job_id in self.jobs:
                del self.jobs[job_id]
                file_path = os.path.join(self.storage_path, f"{job_id}.json")
                if os.path.exists(file_path):
                    os.remove(file_path)
                return True
        return False


# Global singleton instance
job_service = JobService()

import redis.asyncio as redis
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any
import json
from config import settings


class QueueService:
    def __init__(self):
        self.redis_client: Optional[redis.Redis] = None
        self.queue_name = "pdf_jobs"
    
    async def connect(self):
        """Connect to Redis."""
        self.redis_client = await redis.Redis(
            host=settings.REDIS_HOST,
            port=settings.REDIS_PORT,
            db=settings.REDIS_DB,
            password=settings.REDIS_PASSWORD,
            decode_responses=True
        )
        await self.redis_client.ping()
    
    async def disconnect(self):
        """Disconnect from Redis."""
        if self.redis_client:
            await self.redis_client.close()
    
    async def enqueue_job(self, job_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Add a job to the queue."""
        try:
            await self.redis_client.lpush(self.queue_name, json.dumps(job_data))
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error enqueuing job: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def dequeue_job(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get a job from the queue (blocking)."""
        try:
            result = await self.redis_client.brpop(self.queue_name, timeout=5)
            if result:
                _, job_data = result
                return json.loads(job_data)
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error dequeuing job: {e}")
            return None
    
    async def get_queue_length(self) -> int:
        """Get the current queue length."""
        try:
            return await self.redis_client.llen(self.queue_name)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error getting queue length: {e}")
            return 0
    
    async def clear_queue(self) -> bool:
        """Clear all jobs from the queue."""
        try:
            await self.redis_client.delete(self.queue_name)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error clearing queue: {e}")
            return False


# Global singleton instance
queue_service = QueueService()

import os
import re
import uuid
import shutil
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

# ØªØ­Ø¯ÙŠØ¯ Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø¬Ù„Ø¯Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„Ø£Ø³Ø§Ø³ÙŠØ© Ù„Ù„ØªØ®Ø²ÙŠÙ† Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø¤Ù‚Øª
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
STORAGE_DIR = BASE_DIR / "storage"
UPLOAD_DIR = STORAGE_DIR / "uploads"
OUTPUT_DIR = STORAGE_DIR / "outputs"
JOBS_DIR = STORAGE_DIR / "jobs"

# Ø§Ù„Ø§Ù…ØªØ¯Ø§Ø¯Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø¯Ø¹ÙˆÙ…Ø© ÙÙŠ Ø§Ù„Ù…Ù†ØµØ©
ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {
    "merge": {".pdf"},
    "split": {".pdf"},
    "compress": {".pdf"},
    "rotate": {".pdf"},
    "organize": {".pdf"},
    "pdf-to-jpg": {".pdf"},
    "jpg-to-pdf": {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png"},
    "watermark": {".pdf"},
    "protect": {".pdf"},
    "unlock": {".pdf"},

def ensure_storage_dirs() -> None:
    """Ø§Ù„ØªØ£ÙƒØ¯ Ù…Ù† ÙˆØ¬ÙˆØ¯ Ù…Ø¬Ù„Ø¯Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„ØªØ®Ø²ÙŠÙ† Ø§Ù„Ø£Ø³Ø§Ø³ÙŠØ© Ø¹Ù†Ø¯ Ø¥Ù‚Ù„Ø§Ø¹ Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø§Ø¯Ù…."""

    """
    # Ø§Ø³ØªØ®Ø±Ø§Ø¬ Ø§Ù„Ø§Ø³Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø£Ø³Ø§Ø³ÙŠ Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø³Ø§Ø±
    clean_name = os.path.basename(filename)

    clean_name = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9_.-]', '_', clean_name)

def validate_file_extension(filename: str, operation: str) -> bool:
    """Ø§Ù„ØªØ­Ù‚Ù‚ Ù…Ù† Ø£Ù† Ø§Ù…ØªØ¯Ø§Ø¯ Ø§Ù„Ù…Ù„Ù Ù…ØªÙˆØ§ÙÙ‚ Ù…Ø¹ Ù†ÙˆØ¹ Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ù…Ù„ÙŠØ© Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø·Ù„ÙˆØ¨Ø©."""
    ext = Path(filename).suffix.lower()
    allowed = ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS.get(operation, {".pdf"})
    return ext in allowed


def get_job_upload_path(job_id: str, filename: str) -> Path:
    """ØªØ­Ø¯ÙŠØ¯ Ù…Ø³Ø§Ø± Ø­ÙØ¸ Ø§Ù„Ù…Ù„Ù Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø±ÙÙˆØ¹ Ø¯Ø§Ø®Ù„ Ù…Ø¬Ù„Ø¯ Ø®Ø§Øµ Ø¨ÙƒÙ„ Ù…Ù‡Ù…Ø©."""

def get_job_output_path(job_id: str, filename: str) -> Path:
    """ØªØ­Ø¯ÙŠØ¯ Ù…Ø³Ø§Ø± Ø­ÙØ¸ Ø§Ù„Ù…Ù„Ù Ø§Ù„Ù†Ø§ØªØ¬ Ø¨Ø¹Ø¯ Ø¥ØªÙ…Ø§Ù… Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø¹Ø§Ù„Ø¬Ø©."""

def format_file_size(size_in_bytes: int) -> str:
    """ØªØ­ÙˆÙŠÙ„ Ø­Ø¬Ù… Ø§Ù„Ù…Ù„Ù Ù…Ù† Bytes Ø¥Ù„Ù‰ ØµÙŠØºØ© Ù‚Ø§Ø¨Ù„Ø© Ù„Ù„Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø¡Ø© (KB, MB)."""
    for unit in ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB']:
        if size_in_bytes < 1024.0:
            return f"{size_in_bytes:.2f} {unit}"
        size_in_bytes /= 1024.0
    return f"{size_in_bytes:.2f} TB"


def cleanup_expired_files(max_age_hours: int = 2) -> int:
    """
    Ø­Ø°Ù ØªÙ„Ù‚Ø§Ø¦ÙŠ Ù„ÙƒØ§ÙØ© Ø§Ù„Ù…Ù„ÙØ§Øª ÙˆØ§Ù„Ù…Ø¬Ù„Ø¯Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„ØªÙŠ ØªØ¬Ø§ÙˆØ² Ø¹Ù…Ø±Ù‡Ø§ Ø³Ø§Ø¹ØªÙŠÙ†
    Ù„Ø¶Ù…Ø§Ù† Ø§Ù„Ø­ÙØ§Ø¸ Ø¹Ù„Ù‰ Ø§Ù„Ø®ØµÙˆØµÙŠØ© ÙˆØªÙˆÙÙŠØ± Ù…Ø³Ø§Ø­Ø© Ø§Ù„ØªØ®Ø²ÙŠÙ†.
    """
    now = datetime.now()
    expiration_limit = now - timedelta(hours=max_age_hours)
    deleted_count = 0

    for target_dir in [UPLOAD_DIR, OUTPUT_DIR, JOBS_DIR]:
        if not target_dir.exists():
            continue

        for item in target_dir.iterdir():
            try:
                item_stat = item.stat()
                mtime = datetime.fromtimestamp(item_stat.st_mtime)

                if mtime < expiration_limit:
                    if item.is_dir():
                        shutil.rmtree(item, ignore_errors=True)
                    else:
                        item.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                    deleted_count += 1
            except Exception:
                # ØªØ¬Ø§Ù‡Ù„ Ø§Ù„Ø£Ø®Ø·Ø§Ø¡ Ø£Ø«Ù†Ø§Ø¡ Ø§Ù†Ø´ØºØ§Ù„ Ø§Ù„Ù…Ù„Ù Ø¨Ø¹Ù…Ù„ÙŠØ© Ø£Ø®Ø±Ù‰
                continue

    return deleted_count

import os
from typing import Optional
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    # API Settings
    API_PREFIX: str = "/api/v1"
    API_TITLE: str = "MyPDF API"
    API_VERSION: str = "1.0.0"
    
    # Server Settings
    HOST: str = "0.0.0.0"
    PORT: int = 8080
    
    # CORS Settings
    CORS_ORIGINS: list = ["*"]
    CORS_ALLOW_CREDENTIALS: bool = True
    CORS_ALLOW_METHODS: list = ["*"]
    CORS_ALLOW_HEADERS: list = ["*"]
    
    # Redis Settings
    REDIS_HOST: str = "localhost"
    REDIS_PORT: int = 6379
    REDIS_DB: int = 0
    REDIS_PASSWORD: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Database Settings
    DATABASE_URL: str = "sqlite+aiosqlite:///./database/app.db"
    
    # Storage Settings
    STORAGE_PATH: str = "C:\\Users\\ADIL\\Documents\\pj\\storage"
    UPLOADS_PATH: str = "C:\\Users\\ADIL\\Documents\\pj\\storage\\uploads"
    OUTPUTS_PATH: str = "C:\\Users\\ADIL\\Documents\\pj\\storage\\outputs"
    JOBS_PATH: str = "C:\\Users\\ADIL\\Documents\\pj\\storage\\jobs"
    
    # Job Settings
    JOB_EXPIRATION_HOURS: int = 2
    MAX_FILE_SIZE: int = 100 * 1024 * 1024  # 100MB
    
    # Security Settings
    SECRET_KEY: str = "your-secret-key-change-in-production"
    ALGORITHM: str = "HS256"
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = 30
    
    class Config:
        env_file = ".env"
        case_sensitive = True


settings = Settings()

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import create_async_engine, AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base
from config import settings

# Create async engine
engine = create_async_engine(
    settings.DATABASE_URL,
    echo=False,
    future=True
)

# Create async session factory
async_session = async_sessionmaker(
    engine,
    class_=AsyncSession,
    expire_on_commit=False
)

# Base class for models
Base = declarative_base()


async def get_db() -> AsyncSession:
    """Dependency for getting async database sessions."""
    async with async_session() as session:
        try:
            yield session
        finally:
            await session.close()


async def init_db():
    """Initialize database tables."""
    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)


async def close_db():
    """Close database connections."""
    await engine.dispose()

FILE: C:\Users\ADIL\Documents\pj\api-fastapi\main.py
================================================

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from config import settings
from database import init_db, close_db
from services import queue_service
from routers import health_router, upload_router, jobs_router, download_router


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    """Lifespan context manager for startup and shutdown events."""
    # Startup
    await init_db()
    await queue_service.connect()
    
    yield
    
    # Shutdown
    await queue_service.disconnect()
    await close_db()


app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.API_TITLE,
    version=settings.API_VERSION,
    lifespan=lifespan
)

# CORS - allowing all origins
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=settings.CORS_ORIGINS,
    allow_credentials=settings.CORS_ALLOW_CREDENTIALS,
    allow_methods=settings.CORS_ALLOW_METHODS,
    allow_headers=settings.CORS_ALLOW_HEADERS,
)

# Include routers with /api/v1 prefix
app.include_router(health_router, prefix=settings.API_PREFIX, tags=["health"])
app.include_router(upload_router, prefix=settings.API_PREFIX, tags=["upload"])
app.include_router(jobs_router, prefix=settings.API_PREFIX, tags=["jobs"])
app.include_router(download_router, prefix=settings.API_PREFIX, tags=["download"])


@app.get("/")
async def root():
    """Root endpoint."""
    return {
        "message": settings.API_TITLE,
        "version": settings.API_VERSION,
        "docs": "/docs",
        "health": f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/health"
    }

    // ÙƒØ´Ù Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø³Ø§Ø± Ø§Ù„Ù†Ø³Ø¨ÙŠ Ø§Ù„ØµØ­ÙŠØ­ Ø­Ø³Ø¨ Ù…ÙƒØ§Ù† ØªÙˆØ§Ø¬Ø¯ ØµÙØ­Ø© Ø§Ù„Ù€ HTML
    getBasePath() {
        return window.location.pathname.includes('/pages/') ? '../' : './';
    }

    async init() {
        await this.loadTranslations(this.currentLang);
        this.applyLanguage();
    }

    async loadTranslations(lang) {
        const basePath = this.getBasePath();
        try {
            const response = await fetch(`${basePath}locales/${lang}.json`);
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
            this.translations = await response.json();
        } catch (error) {
            // Ø§Ø¹ØªÙ…Ø§Ø¯ Ø§Ù„Ù‚Ø§Ù…ÙˆØ³ Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø¯Ù…Ø¬ Ø¹Ù†Ø¯ ÙØ´Ù„ Ø§Ù„Ù€ fetch Ù„ØªÙØ§Ø¯ÙŠ Ø¥Ø¸Ù‡Ø§Ø± Ø§Ù„Ù…ÙØ§ØªÙŠØ­
            this.translations = this.fallbackTranslations[lang] || this.fallbackTranslations['en'];
        }
    }

    async setLanguage(lang) {
        if (lang === this.currentLang) return;
        
        this.currentLang = lang;
        localStorage.setItem('mypdf_lang', lang);
        await this.loadTranslations(lang);
        this.applyLanguage();
    }

    applyLanguage() {
        document.documentElement.dir = this.currentLang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
        document.documentElement.lang = this.currentLang;

        const basePath = this.getBasePath();
        let rtlLink = document.getElementById('rtl-css');

        if (this.currentLang === 'ar') {
            if (!rtlLink) {
                rtlLink = document.createElement('link');
                rtlLink.id = 'rtl-css';
                rtlLink.rel = 'stylesheet';
                rtlLink.href = `${basePath}css/rtl.css`;
                document.head.appendChild(rtlLink);
            }
        } else if (rtlLink) {
            rtlLink.remove();
        }

        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(element => {
            const key = element.getAttribute('data-i18n');
            const translation = this.getTranslation(key);
            if (translation) {
                element.textContent = translation;
            }
        });

        const langToggle = document.getElementById('langToggle');
        if (langToggle) {
            langToggle.textContent = this.currentLang === 'ar' ? 'English' : 'Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ø±Ø¨ÙŠØ©';
        }

        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('languageChanged', { 
            detail: { lang: this.currentLang } 
        }));
    }

    getTranslation(key) {
        const keys = key.split('.');
        let value = this.translations;
        
        for (const k of keys) {
            if (value && typeof value === 'object' && k in value) {
                value = value[k];
            } else {
                // Ù…Ø­Ø§ÙˆÙ„Ø© Ø§Ù„Ø¨Ø­Ø« Ø¯Ø§Ø®Ù„ Ø§Ù„Ù‚Ø§Ù…ÙˆØ³ Ø§Ù„Ø§Ø­ØªÙŠØ§Ø·ÙŠ
                const fallback = this.fallbackTranslations[this.currentLang] || this.fallbackTranslations['en'];
                return fallback[key] || key;
            }
        }
        
        return value;
    }

    t(key) {
        return this.getTranslation(key);
    }
}

const i18n = new I18n();
window.i18n = i18n;

                <i class="fa-solid fa-download"></i> Download Result

    <script src="../js/i18n.js"></script>
    <script>
        const dropzone = document.getElementById('dropzone');
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const compressBtn = document.getElementById('compressBtn');
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearBtn');
        const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
        const progressFill = document.getElementById('progressFill');
        const progressText = document.getElementById('progressText');
        const resultContainer = document.getElementById('resultContainer');
        const downloadBtn = document.getElementById('downloadBtn');
        const langToggle = document.getElementById('langToggle');
        const alertBox = document.getElementById('statusAlert');

        let selectedFile = null;
        let currentJobId = null;

        // Language toggle
        langToggle.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const newLang = i18n.currentLang === 'en' ? 'ar' : 'en';
            await i18n.setLanguage(newLang);
        });

        // Alert management
        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            alertBox.style.display = 'block';
            alertBox.className = `alert-${type}`;
            alertBox.innerHTML = msg;
        }

        // File handling
        dropzone.addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());
        
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.add('dragover');
        });

        dropzone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => {
            dropzone.classList.remove('dragover');
        });

        dropzone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.remove('dragover');
            const files = e.dataTransfer.files;
            if (files.length > 0 && (files[0].type === 'application/pdf' || files[0].name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf'))) {
                selectedFile = files[0];
                dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><p>${selectedFile.name} (${formatFileSize(selectedFile.size)})</p>`;
                compressBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        fileInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            if (e.target.files.length > 0) {
                selectedFile = e.target.files[0];
                dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><p>${selectedFile.name} (${formatFileSize(selectedFile.size)})</p>`;
                compressBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            selectedFile = null;
            fileInput.value = '';
            dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-cloud-arrow-up"></i><p data-i18n="upload.dragDrop">Drag and drop files here or click to browse</p>`;
            compressBtn.disabled = true;
            resetUI();
        });

        function resetUI() {
            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
            resultContainer.style.display = 'none';
            progressFill.style.width = '0%';
        }

        function formatFileSize(bytes) {
            if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
            const k = 1024;
            const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
            const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
            return Math.round(bytes / Math.pow(k, i) * 100) / 100 + ' ' + sizes[i];
        }

        // Compress functionality
        compressBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (!selectedFile) return;

            resetUI();
            progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
            compressBtn.disabled = true;

            const formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('files', selectedFile);
            formData.append('operation', 'compress');
            formData.append('compression_level', document.getElementById('compressionLevel').value);

            try {
                const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/api/v1/upload', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    body: formData
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (response.ok && data.success) {
                    currentJobId = data.job_id;
                    pollJobStatus(currentJobId);
                } else {
                    showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}: ${data.detail || i18n.t('upload.failed')}`, 'error');
                    compressBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.networkError')}`, 'error');
                compressBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        async function pollJobStatus(jobId) {
            const maxAttempts = 60;
            let attempts = 0;

            const pollInterval = setInterval(async () => {
                attempts++;
                const progress = Math.min((attempts / maxAttempts) * 100, 90);
                progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
                progressText.textContent = `${i18n.t('upload.processing')} (${attempts}/${maxAttempts})`;

                try {
                    const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8080/api/v1/jobs/${jobId}`);
                    const data = await response.json();
                    
                    if (response.ok && data.success) {
                        const job = data.job;
                        const status = job.status;
                        
                        if (status === 'PROCESSING') {
                            // Continue polling
                        } else if (status === 'COMPLETED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            progressFill.style.width = '100%';
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            resultContainer.style.display = 'block';
                            downloadBtn.href = `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/download/${job.output_file}`;
                            
                            // Update compression stats (mock data for now)
                            if (selectedFile) {
                                document.getElementById('originalSize').textContent = formatFileSize(selectedFile.size);
                                const compressedSize = Math.round(selectedFile.size * 0.7); // Mock 30% reduction
                                document.getElementById('compressedSize').textContent = formatFileSize(compressedSize);
                                document.getElementById('reduction').textContent = '30%';
                            }
                            
                            compressBtn.disabled = false;
                        } else if (status === 'FAILED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('status.failed')}: ${job.error || i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            compressBtn.disabled = false;
                        }
                    } else {
                        clearInterval(pollInterval);
                        showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        compressBtn.disabled = false;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Polling error:', err);
                }
                
                if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    showMessage(`â±ï¸ ${i18n.t('errors.timeout')}`, 'error');
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    compressBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            }, 2000);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        const dropzone = document.getElementById('dropzone');
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const fileList = document.getElementById('fileList');
        const convertBtn = document.getElementById('convertBtn');
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearBtn');
        const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
        const progressFill = document.getElementById('progressFill');
        const progressText = document.getElementById('progressText');
        const resultContainer = document.getElementById('resultContainer');
        const downloadBtn = document.getElementById('downloadBtn');
        const langToggle = document.getElementById('langToggle');
        const alertBox = document.getElementById('statusAlert');
        let selectedFiles = [];
        let currentJobId = null;

        langToggle.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const newLang = i18n.currentLang === 'en' ? 'ar' : 'en';
            await i18n.setLanguage(newLang);
        });

        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            alertBox.style.display = 'block';
            alertBox.className = `alert-${type}`;
            alertBox.innerHTML = msg;
        }

        dropzone.addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropzone.classList.add('dragover'); });
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => dropzone.classList.remove('dragover'));
        dropzone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.remove('dragover');
            handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files);
        });

        fileInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            handleFiles(e.target.files);
        });

        function handleFiles(files) {
            for (const file of files) {
                if (file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
                    selectedFiles.push(file);
                }
            }
            updateFileList();
        }

        function updateFileList() {
            fileList.innerHTML = '';
            selectedFiles.forEach((file, index) => {
                const fileItem = document.createElement('div');
                fileItem.className = 'file-item';
                fileItem.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="file-info">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-image"></i>
                        <div>
                            <div class="file-name">${file.name}</div>
                            <div class="file-size">${formatFileSize(file.size)}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button class="remove-btn" onclick="removeFile(${index})"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>
                `;
                fileList.appendChild(fileItem);
            });
            convertBtn.disabled = selectedFiles.length === 0;
        }

        function removeFile(index) {
            selectedFiles.splice(index, 1);
            updateFileList();
        }

        function formatFileSize(bytes) {
            if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
            const k = 1024;
            const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
            const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
            return Math.round(bytes / Math.pow(k, i) * 100) / 100 + ' ' + sizes[i];
        }

        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            selectedFiles = [];
            updateFileList();
            resetUI();
        });

        function resetUI() {
            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
            resultContainer.style.display = 'none';
            progressFill.style.width = '0%';
        }

        convertBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (selectedFiles.length === 0) return;
            resetUI();
            progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
            convertBtn.disabled = true;

            const formData = new FormData();
            selectedFiles.forEach(file => formData.append('files', file));
            formData.append('operation', 'jpg_to_pdf');
            formData.append('page_orientation', document.getElementById('pageOrientation').value);
            formData.append('page_margin', document.getElementById('pageMargin').value);

            try {
                const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/api/v1/upload', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (response.ok && data.success) {
                    currentJobId = data.job_id;
                    pollJobStatus(currentJobId);
                } else {
                    showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}: ${data.detail || i18n.t('upload.failed')}`, 'error');
                    convertBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.networkError')}`, 'error');
                convertBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        async function pollJobStatus(jobId) {
            const maxAttempts = 60;
            let attempts = 0;
            const pollInterval = setInterval(async () => {
                attempts++;
                const progress = Math.min((attempts / maxAttempts) * 100, 90);
                progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
                progressText.textContent = `${i18n.t('upload.processing')} (${attempts}/${maxAttempts})`;

                try {
                    const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8080/api/v1/jobs/${jobId}`);
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (response.ok && data.success) {
                        const job = data.job;
                        if (job.status === 'PROCESSING') {
                        } else if (job.status === 'COMPLETED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            progressFill.style.width = '100%';
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            resultContainer.style.display = 'block';
                            downloadBtn.href = `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/download/${job.output_file}`;
                            convertBtn.disabled = false;
                        } else if (job.status === 'FAILED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('status.failed')}: ${job.error || i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            convertBtn.disabled = false;
                        }
                    } else {
                        clearInterval(pollInterval);
                        showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        convertBtn.disabled = false;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Polling error:', err);
                }
                if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    showMessage(`â±ï¸ ${i18n.t('errors.timeout')}`, 'error');
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    convertBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            }, 2000);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        const dropzone = document.getElementById('dropzone');
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const fileList = document.getElementById('fileList');
        const mergeBtn = document.getElementById('mergeBtn');
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearBtn');
        const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
        const progressFill = document.getElementById('progressFill');
        const progressText = document.getElementById('progressText');
        const resultContainer = document.getElementById('resultContainer');
        const downloadBtn = document.getElementById('downloadBtn');
        const langToggle = document.getElementById('langToggle');
        const alertBox = document.getElementById('statusAlert');

        let selectedFiles = [];
        let currentJobId = null;

        // Language toggle
        langToggle.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const newLang = i18n.currentLang === 'en' ? 'ar' : 'en';
            await i18n.setLanguage(newLang);
        });

        // Alert management
        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            alertBox.style.display = 'block';
            alertBox.className = `alert-${type}`;
            alertBox.innerHTML = msg;
        }

        // File handling
        dropzone.addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());
        
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.add('dragover');
        });

        dropzone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => {
            dropzone.classList.remove('dragover');
        });

        dropzone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.remove('dragover');
            handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files);
        });

        fileInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            handleFiles(e.target.files);
        });

        function handleFiles(files) {
            for (const file of files) {
                if (file.type === 'application/pdf' || file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf')) {
                    selectedFiles.push(file);
                }
            }
            updateFileList();
        }

        function updateFileList() {
            fileList.innerHTML = '';
            selectedFiles.forEach((file, index) => {
                const fileItem = document.createElement('div');
                fileItem.className = 'file-item';
                fileItem.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="file-info">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i>
                        <div>
                            <div class="file-name">${file.name}</div>
                            <div class="file-size">${formatFileSize(file.size)}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button class="remove-btn" onclick="removeFile(${index})">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i>
                    </button>
                `;
                fileList.appendChild(fileItem);
            });
            mergeBtn.disabled = selectedFiles.length < 2;
        }

        function removeFile(index) {
            selectedFiles.splice(index, 1);
            updateFileList();
        }

        function formatFileSize(bytes) {
            if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
            const k = 1024;
            const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
            const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
            return Math.round(bytes / Math.pow(k, i) * 100) / 100 + ' ' + sizes[i];
        }

        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            selectedFiles = [];
            updateFileList();
            resetUI();
        });

        function resetUI() {
            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
            resultContainer.style.display = 'none';
            progressFill.style.width = '0%';
        }

        // Merge functionality
        mergeBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (selectedFiles.length < 2) return;

            resetUI();
            progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
            mergeBtn.disabled = true;

            const formData = new FormData();
            selectedFiles.forEach(file => formData.append('files', file));
            formData.append('operation', 'merge');

            try {
                const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/api/v1/upload', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    body: formData
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (response.ok && data.success) {
                    currentJobId = data.job_id;
                    pollJobStatus(currentJobId);
                } else {
                    showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}: ${data.detail || i18n.t('upload.failed')}`, 'error');
                    mergeBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.networkError')}`, 'error');
                mergeBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        async function pollJobStatus(jobId) {
            const maxAttempts = 60;
            let attempts = 0;

            const pollInterval = setInterval(async () => {
                attempts++;
                const progress = Math.min((attempts / maxAttempts) * 100, 90);
                progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
                progressText.textContent = `${i18n.t('upload.processing')} (${attempts}/${maxAttempts})`;

                try {
                    const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8080/api/v1/jobs/${jobId}`);
                    const data = await response.json();
                    
                    if (response.ok && data.success) {
                        const job = data.job;
                        const status = job.status;
                        
                        if (status === 'PROCESSING') {
                            // Continue polling
                        } else if (status === 'COMPLETED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            progressFill.style.width = '100%';
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            resultContainer.style.display = 'block';
                            downloadBtn.href = `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/download/${job.output_file}`;
                            mergeBtn.disabled = false;
                        } else if (status === 'FAILED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('status.failed')}: ${job.error || i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            mergeBtn.disabled = false;
                        }
                    } else {
                        clearInterval(pollInterval);
                        showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        mergeBtn.disabled = false;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Polling error:', err);
                }
                
                if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    showMessage(`â±ï¸ ${i18n.t('errors.timeout')}`, 'error');
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    mergeBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            }, 2000);
        }
    </script>

<body>
    <div class="star-bg"><div id="stars"></div><div id="stars2"></div><div id="stars3"></div></div>
    <header>
        <a href="../index.html" class="brand"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf fa-xl"></i><h1 data-i18n="app.name">MyPDF</h1></a>
        <ul class="nav-links">
            <li><a href="tools.html" data-i18n="nav.tools">All Tools</a></li>
            <li><button id="langToggle" class="lang-btn">Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ø±Ø¨ÙŠØ©</button></li>
            <li><a href="../index.html" class="nav-btn" data-i18n="nav.home">Home</a></li>
        </ul>
    </header>
    <div id="statusAlert"></div>
    <div class="tool-container">
        <div class="tool-header">
            <h2 data-i18n="tools.organize.title">Organize PDF</h2>
            <p data-i18n="tools.organize.description">Reorder, delete, or extract pages from your PDF.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="dropzone" id="dropzone"><i class="fa-solid fa-cloud-arrow-up"></i><p data-i18n="upload.dragDrop">Drag and drop files here or click to browse</p></div>
        <input type="file" id="fileInput" accept=".pdf" />
        <div class="page-organizer" id="pageOrganizer">
            <div class="page-list" id="pageList"></div>
            <p style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 14px; text-align: center;">Drag pages to reorder. Use buttons to delete or extract pages.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="action-buttons">
            <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="clearBtn" data-i18n="actions.cancel">Clear</button>
            <button class="btn btn-primary" id="organizeBtn" disabled data-i18n="actions.start">Organize PDF</button>
        </div>
        <div class="progress-container" id="progressContainer">
            <div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" id="progressFill"></div></div>
            <div class="progress-text" id="progressText">Processing...</div>
        </div>
        <div class="result-container" id="resultContainer">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i>
            <h3 data-i18n="tools.organize.success">PDF organized successfully!</h3>
            <a href="#" class="download-btn" id="downloadBtn" data-i18n="actions.download"><i class="fa-solid fa-download"></i> Download Result</a>
        </div>
    </div>
    <script src="../js/i18n.js"></script>
    <script>
        const dropzone = document.getElementById('dropzone');
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const pageOrganizer = document.getElementById('pageOrganizer');
        const pageList = document.getElementById('pageList');
        const organizeBtn = document.getElementById('organizeBtn');
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearBtn');
        const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
        const progressFill = document.getElementById('progressFill');
        const progressText = document.getElementById('progressText');
        const resultContainer = document.getElementById('resultContainer');
        const downloadBtn = document.getElementById('downloadBtn');
        const langToggle = document.getElementById('langToggle');
        const alertBox = document.getElementById('statusAlert');
        let selectedFile = null;
        let currentJobId = null;
        let pages = [];
        let draggedItem = null;

        langToggle.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const newLang = i18n.currentLang === 'en' ? 'ar' : 'en';
            await i18n.setLanguage(newLang);
        });

        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            alertBox.style.display = 'block';
            alertBox.className = `alert-${type}`;
            alertBox.innerHTML = msg;
        }

        dropzone.addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropzone.classList.add('dragover'); });
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => dropzone.classList.remove('dragover'));
        dropzone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.remove('dragover');
            const files = e.dataTransfer.files;
            if (files.length > 0 && (files[0].type === 'application/pdf' || files[0].name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf'))) {
                selectedFile = files[0];
                dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><p>${selectedFile.name}</p>`;
                initializePages();
            }
        });

        fileInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            if (e.target.files.length > 0) {
                selectedFile = e.target.files[0];
                dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><p>${selectedFile.name}</p>`;
                initializePages();
            }
        });

        function initializePages() {
            // Mock page initialization - in real implementation, this would read actual PDF pages
            pages = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) => ({ id: i + 1, number: i + 1, action: 'keep' }));
            renderPages();
            pageOrganizer.classList.add('active');
            organizeBtn.disabled = false;
        }

        function renderPages() {
            pageList.innerHTML = '';
            pages.forEach((page, index) => {
                if (page.action === 'delete') return;
                
                const pageItem = document.createElement('div');
                pageItem.className = 'page-item';
                pageItem.draggable = true;
                pageItem.dataset.index = index;
                pageItem.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="page-number">Page ${page.number}</div>
                    <div class="page-preview"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i></div>
                    <div class="page-actions">
                        <button class="page-action-btn delete" onclick="deletePage(${index})"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button>
                    </div>
                `;
                
                pageItem.addEventListener('dragstart', handleDragStart);
                pageItem.addEventListener('dragend', handleDragEnd);
                pageItem.addEventListener('dragover', handleDragOver);
                pageItem.addEventListener('drop', handleDrop);
                
                pageList.appendChild(pageItem);
            });
        }

        function handleDragStart(e) {
            draggedItem = this;
            this.classList.add('dragging');
        }

        function handleDragEnd(e) {
            this.classList.remove('dragging');
            draggedItem = null;
        }

        function handleDragOver(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (this !== draggedItem) {
                this.style.border = '2px dashed var(--primary)';
            }
        }

        function handleDrop(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.style.border = '1px solid var(--border)';
            
            if (this !== draggedItem) {
                const fromIndex = parseInt(draggedItem.dataset.index);
                const toIndex = parseInt(this.dataset.index);
                
                const [movedPage] = pages.splice(fromIndex, 1);
                pages.splice(toIndex, 0, movedPage);
                
                renderPages();
            }
        }

        function deletePage(index) {
            pages[index].action = 'delete';
            renderPages();
        }

        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            selectedFile = null;
            fileInput.value = '';
            dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-cloud-arrow-up"></i><p data-i18n="upload.dragDrop">Drag and drop files here or click to browse</p>`;
            pageOrganizer.classList.remove('active');
            pages = [];
            organizeBtn.disabled = true;
            resetUI();
        });

        function resetUI() {
            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
            resultContainer.style.display = 'none';
            progressFill.style.width = '0%';
        }

        organizeBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (!selectedFile) return;
            resetUI();
            progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
            organizeBtn.disabled = true;

            const pageOrder = pages.filter(p => p.action !== 'delete').map(p => p.number);
            const formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('files', selectedFile);
            formData.append('operation', 'organize');
            formData.append('page_order', JSON.stringify(pageOrder));

            try {
                const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/api/v1/upload', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (response.ok && data.success) {
                    currentJobId = data.job_id;
                    pollJobStatus(currentJobId);
                } else {
                    showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}: ${data.detail || i18n.t('upload.failed')}`, 'error');
                    organizeBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.networkError')}`, 'error');
                organizeBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        async function pollJobStatus(jobId) {
            const maxAttempts = 60;
            let attempts = 0;
            const pollInterval = setInterval(async () => {
                attempts++;
                const progress = Math.min((attempts / maxAttempts) * 100, 90);
                progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
                progressText.textContent = `${i18n.t('upload.processing')} (${attempts}/${maxAttempts})`;

                try {
                    const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8080/api/v1/jobs/${jobId}`);
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (response.ok && data.success) {
                        const job = data.job;
                        if (job.status === 'PROCESSING') {
                        } else if (job.status === 'COMPLETED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            progressFill.style.width = '100%';
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            resultContainer.style.display = 'block';
                            downloadBtn.href = `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/download/${job.output_file}`;
                            organizeBtn.disabled = false;
                        } else if (job.status === 'FAILED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('status.failed')}: ${job.error || i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            organizeBtn.disabled = false;
                        }
                    } else {
                        clearInterval(pollInterval);
                        showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        organizeBtn.disabled = false;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Polling error:', err);
                }
                if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    showMessage(`â±ï¸ ${i18n.t('errors.timeout')}`, 'error');
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    organizeBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            }, 2000);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="../js/i18n.js"></script>
    <script>
        const dropzone = document.getElementById('dropzone');
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const convertBtn = document.getElementById('convertBtn');
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearBtn');
        const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
        const progressFill = document.getElementById('progressFill');
        const progressText = document.getElementById('progressText');
        const resultContainer = document.getElementById('resultContainer');
        const downloadBtn = document.getElementById('downloadBtn');
        const langToggle = document.getElementById('langToggle');
        const alertBox = document.getElementById('statusAlert');
        let selectedFile = null;
        let currentJobId = null;

        langToggle.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const newLang = i18n.currentLang === 'en' ? 'ar' : 'en';
            await i18n.setLanguage(newLang);
        });

        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            alertBox.style.display = 'block';
            alertBox.className = `alert-${type}`;
            alertBox.innerHTML = msg;
        }

        dropzone.addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropzone.classList.add('dragover'); });
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => dropzone.classList.remove('dragover'));
        dropzone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.remove('dragover');
            const files = e.dataTransfer.files;
            if (files.length > 0 && (files[0].type === 'application/pdf' || files[0].name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf'))) {
                selectedFile = files[0];
                dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><p>${selectedFile.name}</p>`;
                convertBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        fileInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            if (e.target.files.length > 0) {
                selectedFile = e.target.files[0];
                dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><p>${selectedFile.name}</p>`;
                convertBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            selectedFile = null;
            fileInput.value = '';
            dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-cloud-arrow-up"></i><p data-i18n="upload.dragDrop">Drag and drop files here or click to browse</p>`;
            convertBtn.disabled = true;
            resetUI();
        });

        function resetUI() {
            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
            resultContainer.style.display = 'none';
            progressFill.style.width = '0%';
        }

        convertBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (!selectedFile) return;
            resetUI();
            progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
            convertBtn.disabled = true;

            const formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('files', selectedFile);
            formData.append('operation', 'pdf_to_jpg');
            formData.append('image_quality', document.getElementById('imageQuality').value);
            formData.append('image_dpi', document.getElementById('imageDPI').value);

            try {
                const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/api/v1/upload', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (response.ok && data.success) {
                    currentJobId = data.job_id;
                    pollJobStatus(currentJobId);
                } else {
                    showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}: ${data.detail || i18n.t('upload.failed')}`, 'error');
                    convertBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.networkError')}`, 'error');
                convertBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        async function pollJobStatus(jobId) {
            const maxAttempts = 60;
            let attempts = 0;
            const pollInterval = setInterval(async () => {
                attempts++;
                const progress = Math.min((attempts / maxAttempts) * 100, 90);
                progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
                progressText.textContent = `${i18n.t('upload.processing')} (${attempts}/${maxAttempts})`;

                try {
                    const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8080/api/v1/jobs/${jobId}`);
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (response.ok && data.success) {
                        const job = data.job;
                        if (job.status === 'PROCESSING') {
                        } else if (job.status === 'COMPLETED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            progressFill.style.width = '100%';
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            resultContainer.style.display = 'block';
                            downloadBtn.href = `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/download/${job.output_file}`;
                            convertBtn.disabled = false;
                        } else if (job.status === 'FAILED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('status.failed')}: ${job.error || i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            convertBtn.disabled = false;
                        }
                    } else {
                        clearInterval(pollInterval);
                        showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        convertBtn.disabled = false;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Polling error:', err);
                }
                if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    showMessage(`â±ï¸ ${i18n.t('errors.timeout')}`, 'error');
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    convertBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            }, 2000);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="../js/i18n.js"></script>
    <script>
        const dropzone = document.getElementById('dropzone');
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const protectPassword = document.getElementById('protectPassword');
        const confirmPassword = document.getElementById('confirmPassword');
        const passwordStrength = document.getElementById('passwordStrength');
        const protectBtn = document.getElementById('protectBtn');
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearBtn');
        const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
        const progressFill = document.getElementById('progressFill');
        const progressText = document.getElementById('progressText');
        const resultContainer = document.getElementById('resultContainer');
        const downloadBtn = document.getElementById('downloadBtn');
        const generatedPassword = document.getElementById('generatedPassword');
        const langToggle = document.getElementById('langToggle');
        const alertBox = document.getElementById('statusAlert');
        let selectedFile = null;
        let currentJobId = null;

        langToggle.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const newLang = i18n.currentLang === 'en' ? 'ar' : 'en';
            await i18n.setLanguage(newLang);
        });

        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            alertBox.style.display = 'block';
            alertBox.className = `alert-${type}`;
            alertBox.innerHTML = msg;
        }

        function checkPasswordStrength(password) {
            let strength = 0;
            if (password.length >= 8) strength++;
            if (password.length >= 12) strength++;
            if (/[a-z]/.test(password) && /[A-Z]/.test(password)) strength++;
            if (/\d/.test(password)) strength++;
            if (/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/.test(password)) strength++;
            
            if (strength <= 2) return { level: 'weak', text: 'Weak password' };
            if (strength <= 3) return { level: 'medium', text: 'Medium password' };
            return { level: 'strong', text: 'Strong password' };
        }

        protectPassword.addEventListener('input', () => {
            const password = protectPassword.value;
            if (password.length > 0) {
                const strength = checkPasswordStrength(password);
                passwordStrength.className = `password-strength strength-${strength.level}`;
                passwordStrength.textContent = strength.text;
            } else {
                passwordStrength.className = 'password-strength';
                passwordStrength.textContent = '';
            }
            updateProtectButton();
        });

        confirmPassword.addEventListener('input', updateProtectButton);

        function updateProtectButton() {
            const password = protectPassword.value;
            const confirm = confirmPassword.value;
            const hasFile = selectedFile !== null;
            const passwordValid = password.length >= 6 && password === confirm;
            protectBtn.disabled = !hasFile || !passwordValid;
        }

        dropzone.addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropzone.classList.add('dragover'); });
        dropzone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => dropzone.classList.remove('dragover'));
        dropzone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropzone.classList.remove('dragover');
            const files = e.dataTransfer.files;
            if (files.length > 0 && (files[0].type === 'application/pdf' || files[0].name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf'))) {
                selectedFile = files[0];
                dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><p>${selectedFile.name}</p>`;
                updateProtectButton();
            }
        });

        fileInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            if (e.target.files.length > 0) {
                selectedFile = e.target.files[0];
                dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf"></i><p>${selectedFile.name}</p>`;
                updateProtectButton();
            }
        });

        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            selectedFile = null;
            fileInput.value = '';
            protectPassword.value = '';
            confirmPassword.value = '';
            passwordStrength.className = 'password-strength';
            passwordStrength.textContent = '';
            dropzone.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-cloud-arrow-up"></i><p data-i18n="upload.dragDrop">Drag and drop files here or click to browse</p>`;
            protectBtn.disabled = true;
            resetUI();
        });

        function resetUI() {
            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
            resultContainer.style.display = 'none';
            progressFill.style.width = '0%';
        }

        protectBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (!selectedFile || !protectPassword.value) return;
            resetUI();
            progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
            protectBtn.disabled = true;

            const formData = new FormData();
            formData.append('files', selectedFile);
            formData.append('operation', 'protect');
            formData.append('password', protectPassword.value);
            formData.append('encryption_level', document.getElementById('encryptionLevel').value);
            formData.append('allow_print', document.getElementById('permPrint').checked);
            formData.append('allow_copy', document.getElementById('permCopy').checked);
            formData.append('allow_modify', document.getElementById('permModify').checked);

            try {
                const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/api/v1/upload', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
                const data = await response.json();
                if (response.ok && data.success) {
                    currentJobId = data.job_id;
                    generatedPassword.textContent = protectPassword.value;
                    pollJobStatus(currentJobId);
                } else {
                    showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}: ${data.detail || i18n.t('upload.failed')}`, 'error');
                    protectBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.networkError')}`, 'error');
                protectBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        async function pollJobStatus(jobId) {
            const maxAttempts = 60;
            let attempts = 0;
            const pollInterval = setInterval(async () => {
                attempts++;
                const progress = Math.min((attempts / maxAttempts) * 100, 90);
                progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
                progressText.textContent = `${i18n.t('upload.processing')} (${attempts}/${maxAttempts})`;

                try {
                    const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8080/api/v1/jobs/${jobId}`);
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (response.ok && data.success) {
                        const job = data.job;
                        if (job.status === 'PROCESSING') {
                        } else if (job.status === 'COMPLETED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            progressFill.style.width = '100%';
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            resultContainer.style.display = 'block';
                            downloadBtn.href = `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/download/${job.output_file}`;
                            protectBtn.disabled = false;
                        } else if (job.status === 'FAILED') {
                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                            showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('status.failed')}: ${job.error || i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            protectBtn.disabled = false;
                        }
                    } else {
                        clearInterval(pollInterval);
                        showMessage(`âŒ ${i18n.t('errors.serverError')}`, 'error');
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        protectBtn.disabled = false;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Polling error:', err);
                }
                if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    showMessage(`â±ï¸ ${i18n.t('errors.timeout')}`, 'error');
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    protectBtn.disabled = false;
                }
            }, 2000);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
